147 /**
148 @ingroup kumi_generators
149
150 @var compress
151 @brief Callable object converting a product type of product type into a single product type recursively, or returns
152 the input.
153
154 This construction naturally arises from the definition of product types, a product type of the form T(T(x...)) is
155 strictly equivalent to T(x...) as long as the T is the same type.
156
157 On record types, the names of the outer record are concatenated to the inner ones ultimately constructing names
158 such as "outer.inner". If the input is a product type containing record types or vice versa only the inner types
159 matching the outer semantic will be compressed. Thus a record inside a tuple will not be compressed.
160
161 @qualifier nodiscard
162 @qualifier inline
163 @qualifier constexpr
164
165 @groupheader{Header file}
166 @code
167 #include <kumi/algorithm/flatten.hpp>
168 @endcode
169
170 @groupheader{Call Signature}
171
172 @code
173 template<product_type T>
174 constexpr auto compress(T && t) noexcept;
175 @endcode
176
177 @subgroupheader{Parameters}
178
179 - `t`: Product type to process
180
181 @subgroupheader{Return value}
182
183 - The resulting value of the application of `T0(...(Tn(values))) => T(values)`
184
185 @groupheader{Helper type}
186
187 @snippet include/kumi/algorithm/flatten.hpp compress_t
188
189 Computes the return type of a call to kumi::compress
190
191 @groupheader{Examples}
192
193 @tab_begin
194
195 @tab{Tuple}
196 @godbolt{doc/tuple/algo/compress.cpp}
197
198 @tab{Record}
199 @godbolt{doc/record/algo/compress.cpp}
200
201 @tab_end
202 **/
203 //====================================================================================================================
204 inline constexpr compress_t compress{};

263 /**
264 @ingroup kumi_generators
265
266 @var flatten_all
267 @brief Callable object converting recursively a product type of product types into a product type of all elements.
268
269 Recursively converts a product type of product types `t` into a product type of all elements of
270 said product types.
271
272 If the Callable object f is provided, non-product type elements are processed by `f` before
273 being inserted.
274
275 On record types, the names of the outer record are concatenated to the inner ones ultimately constructing names
276 such as "outer.inner". If the input is a product type containing record types or vice versa only the inner types
277 matching the outer semantic will be flattened. Thus a record inside a tuple will not be flattened.
278
279 @qualifier nodiscard
280 @qualifier inline
281 @qualifier constexpr
282
283 @groupheader{Header file}
284 @code
285 #include <kumi/algorithm/flatten.hpp>
286 @endcode
287
288 @groupheader{Call Signature}
289
290 @code
291 template<product_type T, typename Function>
292 constexpr auto flatten(T && t, Function && f) noexcept; // 1
293 @endcode
294
295 @code
296 template<product_type T>
297 constexpr auto flatten(T && t) noexcept; // 2
298 @endcode
299
300 @subgroupheader{Parameters}
301
302 - `t`: Product type to process
303 - `f`: Optional Callable object to apply when a sub-tuple is flattened
304
305 @subgroupheader{Return value}
306
307 - A product type composed of all elements of `t` flattened recursively
308
309 @groupheader{Helper type}
310
